## Semantic Chunks
### Section: Introduction
- **Level**: `intro`
- **Hash**: `b161dbc6c65bce4cdcfcf7996bf1c022039e9640dd3dffdce840d18d95a369f4`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/an-ai-detector-flagged-an-essay-but-the-revision-history-looks-normal-which-should-i-trust` (Similarity: 0.9319)

```text
When an AI detector score and document writing history disagree, educators must prioritize physical writing history as the objective ground truth—evaluating active typing duration, revision depth, and oral defenses to resolve the discrepancy.

In modern grading workflows, teachers occasionally encounter two perplexing contradictions: (1) an essay receives an 85% AI score, but the writing history shows 4.5 hours of active keystrokes and 25% backspaces, or (2) an essay receives a 0% AI score, but the writing history shows an entire 1,500-word paper appeared in a single 1-second paste. Resolving these discrepancies requires understanding what each signal measures and relying on Checkmark Plagiarism's Combined Report to guide fair academic decisions.

Below is an educator's decision framework for resolving conflicting integrity evidence.

Checkmark Plagiarism powers multi-signal resolution by pairing essay writing playback with AI detection , plagiarism detection , autograding , and integrations with Canvas and Google Classroom .
```

### Section: The Two Conflict Scenarios & How to Resolve Them
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `39a9a0702fecb9c0eff0b90abb6cf2fc3d576a338007abe7fe6d3e49e8e7ee5d`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-do-i-check-an-assignment-for-both-ai-and-plagiarism` (Similarity: 0.8359)

```text
Scenario A: High AI Score + Robust Writing History

Diagnosis: False Positive. The student writes in a formal, highly structured academic tone that triggered the detector, but 4+ hours of active typing and revisions prove authentic human authorship.

Scenario B: Low AI Score + Instant 1-Second Paste

Diagnosis: External Paste / Plagiarism. The text was not generated by ChatGPT, but copied from a classmate, purchased from a paper mill, or drafted offline in Word. Check the Plagiarism match report.
```

### Section: Why Process Evidence Outranks Statistical AI Scores
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `ae9d1f615356794b3c76a60b0b9a3e72b276312c5f1a92b9e43170f93036b824`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-google-docs-prove-that-a-student-wrote-an-essay` (Similarity: 0.8957)

```text
In the hierarchy of forensic evidence, physical process logs always supersede statistical text predictions:

Statistical AI Detectors: Measure mathematical probability based on training data. They can be fooled by formal grammar or altered prompts. 
 Keystroke Writing Playback: Records physical keypresses, timestamped deletions, and formulation pauses inside the document. Physical reality cannot be simulated. 
 The Rule of Ground Truth: If writing playback proves multi-hour human composition with healthy backspace rates (15–30%), the AI detector score must be disregarded.

Read more in how Checkmark writing process analysis works .
```

### Section: Comparison: Resolving Integrity Conflicts
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `c9aa994db5ba75e47b6f8720a73b733eabde41b5be8e65331810617d547b3b37`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-document-history-protect-a-student-from-a-false-ai-accusation` (Similarity: 0.8688)

```text
High AI (85%) + High Process (4 hrs typing)

Evidence: Multi-session draft, 24% backspaces, verified citations. 
 Cause: Articulate student writing in formal academic syntax. 
 Action: Clear student of all suspicion; grade paper on merit.

Low AI (5%) + Zero Process (<3 min drafting)

Evidence: Blank page to 1,500 words in 1 second; 0% typing. 
 Cause: Peer copying, contract cheating, or uncredited paste. 
 Action: Review plagiarism database matches & request external files.
```

### Section: A 5-Step Decision Protocol for Conflicting Reports
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `c0755af4ec358a090f5fe9a63171f7959fa053d3c0da2403795a76d4851f7aa7`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-should-teachers-interpret-conflicting-ai-detection-results` (Similarity: 0.8780)

```text
Educator Conflict Resolution Checklist:

1. Open the Checkmark Combined Report in Canvas SpeedGrader or Google Classroom. 
 2. Compare the AI Probability Score directly against the Active Typing Hours and Backspace Rate. 
 3. If active typing exceeds 3 hours and backspaces exceed 15% → dismiss the high AI score. 
 4. If active typing is under 5 minutes → investigate the paste origin via Plagiarism Matching. 
 5. Conduct a 2-minute oral check-in to confirm conceptual understanding and close the inquiry.
```
